4691. (l ◇ I) ◇ ll = (lI ◇ ll) ◇ I
4692. (l ◇ I) ◇ ll = (lI ◇ ll) ◇ Il
4693. (l ◇ I) ◇ ll = (lI ◇ Il) ◇ ll
4694. (l ◇ I) ◇ ll = (lI ◇ Il) ◇ II
